The Moonglade Marsh is a [[Locations|Location]] and the first island of [[Galatea]]. It is primarily [[Foraging]], [[Hunting]], and [[Fishing]]-oriented location, with a variety of unique features resolving around progress in those skills. It can be accessed from the [[Jungle Island]] of [[The Park]] after reaching [[Foraging|Foraging XII (12)]].
## Features
The Moonglade Marsh is a [[Foraging]], [[Hunting]], and [[Fishing]]-oriented location, with a variety of unique features resolving around progress in those skills.
When joining the island, the player can first speak to [[Hina]] to unlock the [[Heart of the Forest]], which grants perks that buff [[Foraging]]. The island contains Fig Trees and Mangrove Trees, as well as unique mechanics for those trees. These can be harvested in exchange for Tree Gifts, which are granted to players who helped break the tree.
Players can speak to [[David Hunterborough]] to learn about [[Hunting]] through his Quest. The island contains various Critters, which can be hunted in exchange for [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]]. The [[Heart of the Forest]] also grants Hunting-related perks.
The island also features large bodies of water, which can be swam in or used for [[Fishing]] for exclusive Items or Sea Creatures.
### Chapters
The Moonglade Marsh contains [[Chapters]], which consist of 69 tasks for the player to complete in exchange for rewards. These chapters can be accessed by speaking to [[Hina]].

### Heart of the Forest
The [[Heart of the Forest]] is a [[Skill Tree]] that grants various perks related to [[Foraging]] and [[Hunting]] that is unlocked by speaking to [[Hina]] at [[Tangleburg's Path]]. One can obtain [[Heart of the Forest|HotF EXP]] through [[Tree Gifts]] and [[Agatha|Agatha Contests]], and leveling up the [[Heart of the Forest]] or its Center of the Forest perk grants [[Token of the Forest|Tokens of the Forest]].
### Foraging Mechanics
The Moonglade Marsh contains Fig Trees and Mangrove Trees, which grant [[Fig Log|Fig Logs]] and [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Logs]] respectively. These trees have [[Toughness]] which controls how much [[Sweep]] is required to cut them down. Having more [[Sweep]] allows the player to cut these trees down faster.
These trees also have unique styles, which dictate the order in which they must be cut down Failing to cut down a tree in the correct order results in a 50% [[Sweep]] reduction.
Some Fig and Mangrove Trees have Tree Protection Orders on them. These trees cannot be broken. Instead, the player can right-click them with [[Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Fun-Sized]], [[Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Family-Sized]], [[Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb|Jumbo]], or [[Behemoth Pot of Honeycomb]]. After waiting for a period of time based on the pot used, a random creature will spawn by the tree, depending on the type of tree.
|Tree|Location|Toughness|Style|Tree Protection Order Mobs|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Fig Tree ([[Fig Log]])|Upper area of the Moonglade Marsh|10 [[Toughness]]|Trunk → Branches|[[Honeyhog]], [[Honeymite]], or [[Hewver]]|
|Mangrove Tree ([[Mangrove Log]])|[[Murkwater Loch]]|50 [[Toughness]]|Branches → Trunk → Roots|[[Stag Beetle]], [[Woodlouse]], or [[Murkbat]]|
Fully chopping down a tree rewards the players who helped chop it down with a [[Tree Gifts|Tree Gift]]. The quantity of guaranteed rewards and chance for rare rewards scales based on the amount a player contributes to chopping down a tree, as well as the tree's size.
|Percent Cut|Rewards|
|-|-|
|10%|0 rewards|
|20%|50% of rewards|
|33%|100% of rewards|
The tree size multiplier can range from 1x to 20x.

### Collections
The Moonglade Marsh allows the player to unlock specific new [[Collections]], including [[Fig Log|Fig Logs]], [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Logs]], [[Lushlilac|Lushlilacs]], [[Sea Lumies]], [[Tender Wood]], and [[Vinesap]].
[[Fig Log|Fig Logs]] and [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Logs]] are primarily obtained from cutting down trees. [[Tender Wood]] and [[Vinesap]] also have a chance to be obtained from [[Tree Gifts]] from breaking these.
[[Lushlilac|Lushlilacs]] can be obtained from bushes scattered throughout the island that periodically grow berries. Harvesting a bush grants 1 [[Lushlilac]], 25 [[Foraging]] XP, and 100 [[Forest Whispers]]. Additionally, a [[Birries]] has a 10% chance to spawn.
[[Sea Lumies]] can be harvested from within the [[Murkwater Shallows]] and [[Murkwater Depths]].
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|500|[[Tiny Scaffolding]] Recipe|+4|
|II|1,000|[[Enchanted Fig Log]] Recipe|+4|

### Hunting Quest
When players first join the Moonglade Marsh, they are tasked with completing a quest that introduces them to [[Hunting]]. This involves speaking to [[Swoop]], [[David Hunterborough]], [[Alan]], [[Oden]], [[Albert]], [[Jaeger]], [[Auryon]], [[Kysha]], [[Agatha]], and [[Dax]]. Throughout this quest, the player obtains a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]], [[Lassos|Lasso]], [[Huntaxes|Huntaxe]], [[Black Holes|Pocket Black Hole]], and [[David's Cloak]].
### Starlyn Contests
Within the center of the island in the [[Murkwater Loch]], players can find and speak to [[Agatha]] to learn about [[Agatha|Starlyn Contests]]. These are contests that occur every 20 minutes, where the player can earn points for [[Foraging]] and [[Hunting]] mobs, with more points granting better rewards.
### Fishing Mechanics
Players can go [[Fishing]] on the Moonglade Marsh and catch items from a unique loot pool or unique [[Sea Creatures]].
#### Items
These items can be caught in the Moonglade Marsh.
Additionally, [[Swamp the Fish]] has a 0.05% (1 in 2,000) chance to replace a non-sea creature catch in Moonglade Marsh.
[[Attributes|Flexible Fisher]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] increases the chance to catch [[Flexbone|Flexbones]] by +2.5-25%.

### Critters
There are various [[Critters]] that can be found on the Moonglade Marsh, all of which are tied to [[Hunting]] and cannot be damaged regularly: While these mobs technically count as Critters, [[Azure]], [[Cod]], [[Salmon]] and [[Verdant]] are currently lacking [[Critters|Critter]] mob type.
|Name|Attribute|Capture Method|
|-|-|-|
|[[Azure]]|[[Azure Shard]] Forest Strength ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]).|
|[[Verdant]]|[[Verdant Shard]] Forest Fishing ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]).|
|[[Cod]]|[[Cod Shard]] Cheapskate ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]). [[Cod]] hit by a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]) gain a Spin meter. Reaching 90% Spin causes it to faint and be captured. If Spin exceeds 100%, it explodes and awards nothing.|
|[[Salmon]]|[[Salmon Shard]] Lost and Found ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]). [[Salmon]] hit by a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]) slow down and gain a ⁂ symbol above its head. After reaching 4 ⁂ symbols, it becomes exhausted and captured. The amount of symbols gained is based on the player's [[Pull]].|
|[[Coralot]]|[[Coralot Shard]] Bucket Lover ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]). Coralots ([[Coralot]]) become startled when a player is near, causing a warning to appear above their head. The player needs to stand still for 8s before capturing them. Throwing a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]) before 8s causes the [[Coralot]] to leave without a reward.|
|[[Joydive]]|[[Joydive Shard]] Deep Diving ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a [[Medium Fishing Net]] or better. Upon being hit with a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]), Joydives ([[Joydive]]) swim into a small cave in the [[Murkwater Depths]]. Once inside, throwing a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]) deals damage.|
|[[Lumisquid]]|[[Lumisquid Shard]] Extreme Pressure ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a [[Medium Fishing Net]] or better. Upon being hit with a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]), Lumisquids ([[Lumisquid]]) apply Blindness ([[Blindness Potion]]) for 2 seconds, teleport to a nearby location, and shrink in size.|
|[[Shellwise]]|[[Shellwise Shard]] Shell ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a [[Turbo Fishing Net]]. The Attribute Shards ([[Attributes]]) gained depend on its maximum health. 1,000 [[Health]] grants 1, 1,500 [[Health]] grants 2, and 2,000 [[Health]] grants 3.|
|[[Spike]]|[[Spike Shard]] Payback ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a [[Turbo Fishing Net]]. Spikes ([[Spike]]) can only be captured from 10 blocks away.|
|[[Birries]]|[[Birries Shard]] Yummy ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]) or Lasso ([[Lassos]]). They have 1 stamina bar.|
|[[Phanpyre]]|[[Phanpyre Shard]] Nocturnal Animal ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a Lasso ([[Lassos]]). Phanpyres ([[Phanpyre]]) have a chance to spawn when chopping a tree on the Moonglade Marsh. They have 1 stamina bar. The chance to spawn a [[Phanpyre]] can be increased with Tree Lurker ([[Attributes]]).|
|[[Phanflare]]|[[Phanflare Shard]] Moonglade Mastery ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a Lasso ([[Lassos]]). Phanflares ([[Phanflare]]) have a chance to spawn when chopping a tree on the Moonglade Marsh. They have 2 stamina bars. The chance to spawn a [[Phanflare]] can be increased with Tree Lurker ([[Attributes]]).|
|[[Dreadwing]]|[[Dreadwing Shard]] Essence of the Forest ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a Lasso ([[Lassos]]). It can also be hit with [[Axes]] and captured with Pocket Black Holes ([[Black Holes]]). Dreadwings ([[Dreadwing]]) have a chance to spawn when chopping a tree on the Moonglade Marsh. They have 5 stamina bars. The chance to spawn a [[Dreadwing]] can be increased with Tree Lurker ([[Attributes]]).|
|[[Invisibug]]|[[Invisibug Shard]] Visitor Bait ([[Attributes]])|Capture with a Lasso ([[Lassos]]).|
|[[Hideonleaf]]|[[Hideonleaf Shard]] Mossy Box ([[Attributes]])|Standing on a [[Hideonleaf]] causes them to open and fire a projectile. The projectile must be hit back 3 times to capture them.|
|[[Mossybit]]|[[Mossybit Shard]] Forest Trap ([[Attributes]])|When clicked, Mossybits ([[Mossybit]]) jump around the [[Murkwater Loch]]. After 4 jumps, they become exhausted and are captured. The jumps a [[Mossybit]] takes before growing tired can be reduced with Frog Legs ([[Attributes]]).|
|[[Bambuleaf]]|[[Bambuleaf Shard]] Strong Arms ([[Attributes]])|Feed 40–44 [[Bamboo]] until it passes out.|
|[[Mochibear]]|[[Mochibear Shard]] Strong Legs ([[Attributes]])|Feed 40–44 [[Bamboo]] until it passes out.|

|[[West Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|None|

|[[Westbound Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Leads to the [[Tranquil Pass]].|None|
|[[Murkwater Outpost]]|A place where players can trade [[Forest Essence]] for various [[Foraging]], [[Hunting]], and other miscellaneous perks.|[[Rosemary]]|
|[[North Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|[[Alan]], [[Kiara]], [[Teddy]], [[Vaeri]]|
|[[North Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Houses the [[Red House]] and leads to the [[Side-Ember Way]].|None|

|[[Moonglade's Edge]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]) and learn how to use [[Huntraps]]. Houses a portal to the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. Also leads to the [[Tranquil Pass]].|[[Dax]]|
|[[Murkwater Loch]]|The center of the Moonglade Marsh, where players can learn about Agatha's Contests ([[Agatha]]) and forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]).|[[Agatha]], [[Jaeger]]|
|[[Murkwater Shallows]]|A place where players can fish, forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]) and [[Sea Lumies]].|[[Nemo]]|
|[[Murkwater Depths]]|An underwater area where players can collect [[Sea Lumies]] and Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Water Creatures. Branches off into the [[Forest Temple]] via the [[Ancient Ruins]], [[Drowned Reliquary]] via the [[Reefguard Pass]], the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]] via the [[Dive-Ember Pass]], the [[Driptoad Delve]] via the [[Driptoad Pass]], the [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]], the [[Squid Cave]], and the Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]]).|None|
|[[Ancient Ruins]]|An underwater entrance to the [[Forest Temple]]. Punch the soul lanterns in ascending order of number of [[Sea Lumies]] next to it to open the gate.|[[Tilus]]|
|[[Forest Temple]]|Donate [[Coins]] and Enchanted Fig Logs ([[Enchanted Fig Log]]) to the Forest Starborn to become a benefactor and gain [[Foraging Fortune]].|None|
|[[Reefguard Pass]]|An underwater path to access the [[Drowned Reliquary]].|None|
|[[Drowned Reliquary]]|An underwater ruin containing Tidetots ([[Tidetot]]), Seacurses ([[Seacurse]]), and Hydrospears ([[Hydrospear]]).|None|
|[[Squid Cave]]|An underwater area found on the path to the Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]]) that contains Lumisquids ([[Lumisquid]]).|None|
|Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]])|A cavern where the [[Jade Dragon]] sells the Jade Dragon Egg ([[Jade Dragon Pet]]).|[[Jade Dragon]]|
|[[Dive-Ember Pass]]|An underwater path to the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]].|None|
|[[Stride-Ember Fissure]]|A lava lake where players can hunt Stridersurfers ([[Stridersurfer]]).|None|
|[[Side-Ember Way]]|A bypass that leads to the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]].|None|
|[[Driptoad Pass]]|An underwater path that connects the [[Driptoad Delve]] and the [[Murkwater Depths]].|None|
|[[Driptoad Delve]]|A pond where the player can fish and enter the [[Murkwater Depths]] from the [[Driptoad Pass]].|None|
|[[Kelpwoven Tunnels]]|An underwater tunnel full of coral where the player can hunt Spikes ([[Spike]]).|None|
|[[Bubbleboost Column]]|An underwater vertical tunnel to quickly travel to [[Tangleburg]] from the [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]] and [[Wyrmgrove Tomb]].|None|
|[[Wyrmgrove Tomb]]|A place where players can Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Chillshots ([[Chillshot]]) and Chillblades ([[Chillblade]]).|None|
|[[Tomb Floodway]]|A place where players can fish up Flexbones ([[Flexbone]]).|[[Martin]]|
|[[Tranquil Pass]]|A tunnel where players can harvest [[Bamboo]].|None|
|[[Tranquility Sanctum]]|A cavern where players can Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Bambuleafs ([[Bambuleaf]]) between Spring and Summer, and Mochibears ([[Mochibear]]) between Autumn and Winter.|None|

# Mount Jerry
Categories: Locations, Events, Season of Jerry, V0.7.5
[Mount Jerry]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Jerry's Workshop]]
Coordinates: 15 110 -52
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): Liquid Hot Magma ([[Defend Jerry's Workshop]]) [[Magma Cube]]
[Metadata]
ID: MOUNT_JERRY
Mount Jerry is a [[Locations|Location]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Mount Jerry, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Mount Jerry
■ The fiery core of the Jerry Island.
■ Watch out for eruptions.
■ Explore the depths.
## Features
At the end of the 23rd of Late Winter, Mount Jerry will begin to erupt, spewing out lava. The volcano will throw out [[Defend Jerry's Workshop|Liquid Hot Magmas]] and [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]] in multiple waves in order to defend the gift piles in [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Gallery
- Mount Jerry's eruption sped up.

# Mountain
Aliases: Carrot Cave, Mountains
Categories: Locations, Article stubs
[Mountain]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Sub-locations: [[Weather Station]]
Fairy Souls: 14
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Coordinates: -12 151 44
[Inhabitants]

The Mountain is a large area behind the [[Village]] on the [[Hub Island]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Mountain, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Mountain
■ Climb to the top!
## Purpose
The Mountain contains various [[NPC|NPCs]]. These include [[Scoop]] in a house near the top of the mountain, [[Romero]] during the 5th part of the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest, and the [[Wizard]] after closing [[The Breach]].
Towards the base of the Mountain, a portal to the [[Dungeon Hub]] can be found. [[Nether Wart]] can be harvested in a cave at the mouth of a waterfall, near the [[Artist's Abode]] and the [[Flower House]].

# Murder House
Categories: Locations
[Murder House]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village Plaza]]
Coordinates: -49 68 -39
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Detective Amog]] [[Joliet]] [[Ramero]]
[Metadata]
ID: MURDER_HOUSE
The Murder House is a sub-location of the [[Village Plaza]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
### Reverse-Murder Investigation
The reverse-murder investigation begins in the Murder House. [[Detective Amog]] appears standing in the center of the room and offers the player a [[Detective Scanner]]. Once all necessary objects have been scanned, [[Detective Amog]] will move around the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], and the player must talk to certain [[NPC|NPCs]] to progress.
After talking to the [[Blacksmith (Rift)|Blacksmith]], the player will need to return to the Murder House and scan the painting. After doings so, the quest will move to [[Taylor's]] and back to the house, where [[Ramero]] will appear, barricaded in. After breaking the barricade by calling for [[Wizardman]], the player must kill them with a [[Tactician's Murder Weapon]], and as a reward, will receive a [[Ring of Broken Love]].
### Second Reverse-Murder Investigation
The second reverse-murder investigation begins in the Mountaintop. [[Detective Amog]] will direct the player through the original quest as the player becomes increasingly exasperated: after which, the player must barricade the Murder House and wait for Wizardman to break the barricade, then kill their past self in order to same Romero.
As a reward, the player receives a [[Ring of Eternal Love]].
## NPCs
There are 3 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Murder House:

|[[Detective Amog]]|[[Detective Amog]]|Murder House; -49.81, 68, -37.218 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here at the start of his first reverse-murder quest.|✕|
|[[Detective Amog]]|[[Detective Amog]]|Murder House; -49.5, 68, -38.718 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during his first reverse-murder quest.|✕|
|[[Detective Amog]]|[[Detective Amog]]|Murder House; -51.12, 68, -40.718 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during his first reverse-murder quest.|✕|
|[[Detective Amog]]|[[Detective Amog]]|Murder House; -49.81, 68, -37.218 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during his second reverse-murder quest.|✕|
|[[Detective Amog]]|[[Detective Amog]]|Murder House; -51.12, 68, -40.718 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during his second reverse-murder quest.|✕|
|[[Joliet]]|[[Joliet]]|Murder House; -49, 68, -37.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during [[Detective Amog]]'s second reverse-murder quest after saving [[Romero]].|✕|
|[[Ramero]]|[[Ramero]]|Murder House; -48.5, 68, -38.312 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears at the end of [[Detective Amog]]'s first reverse-murder quest.|✕|

## Trivia
- Entering the Murder House without progressing through the story will kick the player to the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]] and display the following dialogue: You are a bad person for entering a barricaded house without consent! And please try to follow the story, a lot of effort went into it.

# Murkwater Depths
Categories: Locations
[Murkwater Depths]
Type: [[Hunting]] and [[Fishing]] location
Level: Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: "Moonglade Marsh ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Moonglade Marsh]]
Coordinates: -635 72 0
← Previous Location: [[Murkwater Shallows]]
Next Location →: [[Ancient Ruins]] [[Forest Temple]] [[Reefguard Pass]] [[Drowned Reliquary]] [[Squid Cave]] Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]]) [[Dive-Ember Pass]]
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Verdant]] [[Azure]] [[Cod]] [[Salmon]] [[Birries]] [[Coralot]] [[Shellwise]] [[Joydive]] [[Spike]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Azure Shard]] [[Birries Shard]] [[Cod Shard]] [[Coralot Shard]] [[Joydive Shard]] [[Salmon Shard]] [[Shellwise Shard]] [[Spike Shard]] [[Verdant Shard]]
Resources: [[Sea Lumies]]
The Murkwater Depths are a sub-location of the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Murkwater Depths, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Murkwater Depths
■ Discover the lake and its secrets!
■ Hunt all living creatures!
## Features
The Murkwater Depths is a large pond that stretches into several tunnels and locations. It is meant for [[Fishing]] and [[Hunting]], most notably [[Hunting|Water Hunts]], and houses several mobs that can be hunted with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]].
### Ancient Ruins
The [[Ancient Ruins]] serve as an entrance to the [[Forest Temple]].
### Forest Temple
The [[Forest Temple]] can be accessed by activating all the lanterns in the [[Ancient Ruins]]. Donating [[Coins]] and [[Enchanted Fig Log|Enchanted Fig Logs]] to the [[Forest Temple]] for bonuses.
### Reefguard Pass
The [[Reefguard Pass]] is a path to the [[Drowned Reliquary]].
### Drowned Reliquary
The [[Drowned Reliquary]] contains [[Tidetot|Tidetots]], [[Seacurse|Seacurses]], and [[Hydrospear|Hydrospears]].
### Squid Cave
The [[Squid Cave]] contains [[Lumisquid|Lumisquids]].
### Dragon's Lair
The [[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)|Dragon's Lair]] houses the [[Jade Dragon]], which allows the player to purchase a [[Jade Dragon Pet]].
### Dive-Ember Pass
The [[Dive-Ember Pass]] acts as a path to the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]].
## Fairy Souls
There is 1 [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] found in the Murkwater Depths:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|MM-10|-620|66|32|Secret cave behind [[Tomb Floodway]]|✔|
## Trivia
- The Murkwater Depths are 86 block deep.

# Museum
Aliases: Museum Milestones
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed
[Museum]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to Museum]]
Sub-locations: [[Hexatorum]]
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -73 76 80
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Curator]] [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]] [[The Handler]]
The Museum is a a [[Locations|Location]] found within the [[Village]] on the [[Hub Island]]. It serves as a place for players to donate items in exchange for turning them [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
## Purpose
The Museum allows players to donate items to [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]]. When an accepted item is donated for the first time, the player is rewarded with permanent [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]], and the item becomes [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]], meaning it cannot be traded with anyone outside of the player's [[Co-op]] (if they have one).
More valuable items reward more [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]]. The Museum is split into categories based on [[Skills]], including [[Combat]], [[Farming]], [[Mining]], [[Fishing]], [[Foraging]], [[Dungeoneering]], and [[Hunting]]. There is also a Special Items category, in which items donated do not reward any [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]], nor become become [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]]. This category has a limit of 48 items.
[[Armor]] and [[Equipment]] sets can only be donated as a complete set, meaning individual pieces cannot be donated alone. When an item is donated, its creation date, date of donation, and position number (when below 50,000) can be seen. If an item is one of the first 1,000 created, then its number can be seen above the item when displayed in the Museum in one of the display cases.
Players on [[Stranded]] and [[Bingo]] profiles cannot access the Museum.

### Museum Milestones
For every [[SkyBlock Levels|100 SkyBlock XP]], the player is granted one Museum Milestone. These give a +1% Bits Multiplier and increase your [[Bank]] interest by +2%: some milestones give bonus rewards, like [[Bank|Bank Upgrades]].
Show/Hide
|Milestone|XP Required|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Museum Milestone 1|100 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+1% Bits Multiplier; +2% Bank Interest|
|Museum Milestone 2|200 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+1% Bits Multiplier; +2% Bank Interest|
|Museum Milestone 3|300 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Deluxe Bank Upgrade; Museum Appraisal Service; +1% Bits Multiplier; +2% Bank Interest|
|Museum Milestone 4|400 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+1% Bits Multiplier; +2% Bank Interest|

## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Museum, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Museum
■ Talk to Madame Goldsworth.
■ Donate items to your Museum.
■ Visit other players' Museums.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Donating|You donated your <item> to the Museum!|
|Retrieving|You retrieved your <item> from the Museum! It still counts towards your Museum progress, but not towards your total item value.|
|Milestone|You have earned a reward for donating an item! CLICK HERE to claim it!|
## NPCs
There are 3 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Museum:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Curator]]|[[Curator]]|Museum; 27.5, 68, 33.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to access the Museum and their stored items placed within it.|✕|
|[[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]]|[[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]]|Museum; 33.5, 73, 11.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to the Museum, a place where players can store items in exchange for SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|✕|
|[[The Handler]]|[[The Handler]]|[[Hexatorum]]; 40.5, 72, 1.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to access [[The Hex]].|✕|

## Trivia
- There was a window of time, ending with October 15, 2024 during which it was possible to donate items that shared the name of an armor set as an alternative to the full set of armor. This was possible with: [[Pumpkin]] as [[Sprout Armor|Pumpkin Armor]], [[Cactus]] as [[Cactus Armor]], [[Glacite]] as [[Glacite Armor]], [[Melon]] as [[Tater Armor|Melon Armor]], [[Cropie]] as [[Cropie Armor]], [[Squash]] as [[Squash Armor]], [[Sponge]] as [[Sponge Armor]], [[Sorrow]] as [[Sorrow Armor]] and [[Fermento]] as [[Fermento Armor]].
  - This was not possible with items such as [[Lapis Lazuli]], because its armor set is called [[Lapis Armor]], rather than Lapis Lazuli Armor. Similarly it didn't work with [[Emerald]] because its armor is called [[Emerald Armor|Emerald Armor Helmet]] and not just "Emerald Helmet".
  - Donating these items would soulbound them and store them in the museum.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 25, 2021|The Museum is now accessible.|
|Jan 30, 2026|Relocated and redesigned the Museum.|
|Jan 30, 2026|Changed the Categories in the Museum.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added the Museum.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|
|Oct 27, 2021|The Museum is now accessible.|
|Oct 27, 2021|The [[Curator]], [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]], and item pedestals are not there.|
|Nov 2, 2021|Added the [[Curator]] and [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]] to the Museum.|
|Unknown Date|Removed [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]]'s Abiphone Contact.|
|Mar 19, 2022|Temporarily disabled the Museum.|
|Mar 23, 2022|Re-enabled the Museum.|
|May 31, 2023|0.18.5|Added [[Hollow Armor]], [[Hollow Wand]], all missing Mithril and Gemstone Drills and multiple other Garden tools to the Museum.|
|Jun 21, 2023|Added some missing items to the Museum.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|[[Jinxed Voodoo Doll]] can now be donated to the Museum.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|[[Weirder Tuba]], [[Blaze Hat]], [[Sea Lantern Hat]], and [[Magmafish Hat]] can now be donated to the Museum.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Shrunk the Museum zone slightly to make it easier to use abilities around the area.|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Revamped museum rewards and changed items that can be put in the museum.|
|Oct 21, 2024|Aucton House bid limits are no longer tied to museum progression, and the requirement for Shen's Auction has been reduced from milestone 12 to milestone 10.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Relocated and redesigned the Museum.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Changed the Categories in the Museum.|

[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Leather]] [[Rabbit Hide]] [[Rabbit's Foot]] [[Raw Beef]] [[Raw Mutton]] [[Raw Rabbit]] Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) White Wool ([[Wool]])
Resources: [[Sand]] [[Cactus]] [[Sugar Cane]] [[Cocoa Beans]] Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])
The Mushroom Desert is one of the 2 islands in [[The Farming Islands (disambiguation)|The Farming Islands]] that is located north from [[The Barn]], and has items unique to the area.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Mushroom Desert, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Mushroom Desert
■ A desert with a lot of plant life.
## Location
The Mushroom Desert is part of [[The Farming Islands (disambiguation)|The Farming Islands]]. It is accessed by using the north warp pad at [[The Barn]] on the top of the terraces. The player needs to reach [[Farming|Farming V (5)]] to access to this location.
## Zones
The Mushroom Desert is the only public island where players can get [[Sand]] and [[Cactus|Cacti]] for their [[Collections]]. The island is also divided into multiple areas with each one of them serving a different purpose in the game:
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|Mushroom Desert|The second [[Farming]]-based island, which requires Farming V (5) ([[Farming]]) to enter. It serves as place to harvest [[Sand]], [[Cactus]], [[Sugar Cane]], [[Cocoa Beans]], Red Mushrooms ([[Mushroom]]), and Brown Mushrooms ([[Mushroom]]), kill Rabbits ([[Rabbit]]), [[Sheep]], and Mushroom Cows ([[Mushroom Cow]]), and go [[Fishing]].|[[Farmer Jon]], [[Hunter Ava]]|
|^ [[Desert Settlement]]|The entrance to the island. Contains lots of [[Sand]] and [[Cactus]], as well as several quest NPCs ([[NPC]]).|[[Beth]], [[Friendly Hiker]], [[Hungry Hiker]], [[Mason]]|
|^ [[Desert Mountain]]|Contains a pathway to [[Jake's House]].|None|
|^ [[Jake's House]]|Contains [[Jake]], who sells [[Animal Axes]] and [[Reforge Stones]] in exchange for bringing him certain creatures.|[[Jake]]|
|^ [[The Observatory]] [AHN] (This item or feature is currently only available on the Alpha Hypixel Network.)|Contains the [[Spaceman]].|[[Spaceman]]|
|^ [[Archaeological Site]]|Contains the [[Treasure Hunter]], who sells hints for various treasure locations in exchange for [[Coins]].|[[Treasure Hunter]]|
|^ [[Oasis]]|Contains [[Sugar Cane]] and [[Cocoa Beans]], Rabbits ([[Rabbit]]) and [[Sheep]], and water to go [[Fishing]] from.|None|
|^ [[Shepherd's Keep]]|Contains the [[Shepherd]], who gives the player [[Pelts]] in exchange for bringing him his [[Sheep]]. Also contains [[Sand]] and [[Cactus]].|[[Shepherd]]|
|^ [[Trapper's Den]]|Features [[Talbot]]'s [[Pelts]] shop, [[Tony]]'s Tier XII Farming [[Minions]] shop, [[Trevor]] and his hunting quests, and [[Tammy]], who explains the animals that [[Trevor]] wants hunted.|[[Talbot]], [[Tammy]], [[Tony]], [[Trevor]]|
|^ [[Mushroom Gorge]]|A large area for players to collect Mushrooms ([[Mushroom]]) or kill Mushroom Cows ([[Mushroom Cow]]).|[[Hungry Hiker]], [[Romero]]|
|^ [[Overgrown Mushroom Cave]]|Connected to the [[Mushroom Gorge]], and contains more Mushrooms ([[Mushroom]]).|None|
|^ [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]]|Connected to the [[Mushroom Gorge]], and contains more Mushrooms ([[Mushroom]]). Also contains [[Moby]]'s shop, and a path to the [[Underground Lab]].|[[Moby]]|
|^ [[Underground Lab]]|This location is accessed during [[Beth]]'s Shady Jake ([[Quests]]) quest. It is expanded upon during her Further Investigate Jake ([[Quests]]) quest, which unlocks the [[Crop Analyzer]].|[[Beth]], [[Jake]]|

## Trivia
- The Mushroom Desert is the location with the fifth most [[NPC|NPCs]], after the [[Hub Island|Hub]], [[Crimson Isle]], [[Dwarven Mines]], and the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
- The Mushroom Desert and [[The Barn]] are in the same server. This means the player can travel between the islands without using [[Launch Pad (Structure)|Launch Pads]] through items like the [[Grappling Hook]] and [[Aspect of the End]].
- On the original Mushroom Desert island, there was a launch pad that said "Coming later this year!" located at [[Coordinates|246, 99, -412]].

# Mushroom Gorge
Categories: Locations
[Mushroom Gorge]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Farming V (5) ([[Farming]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: None
Location: [[Mushroom Desert]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Hungry Hiker]] [[Romero]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Mushroom Cow]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Leather]] [[Raw Beef]] Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])
Resources: [[Sand]] Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])
The Mushroom Gorge is a location in the [[Mushroom Desert]]. It is mostly located in caves and is one of the biggest locations on the island. One of the entrances to it is located at [[Coordinates|205, 76, -480]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Mushroom Gorge, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Mushroom Gorge
■ Help out the Hungry Hiker.
■ Farm mushrooms.
## Location
The Mushroom Gorge is a large semi-underground location. It is characterized by various [[Mushroom]] trees players can mine to receive [[Mushroom|Mushrooms]]. The area, unlike many locations of the [[Mushroom Desert]], has no obtainable [[Cactus|Cacti]] in it.
### Functions
The area can be used by players to get multiple resources like [[Mushroom]], [[Sand]], [[Raw Beef]] and [[Leather]]. It can also be used to interact with [[Hungry Hiker]], however, his quest does not reward the player.
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Mushroom Gorge:

|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hungry Hiker]]|[[Hungry Hiker]]|Mushroom Gorge; 269.5, 48, -480.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Tasks the player with bringing him food.|✕|
|[[Romero]]|[[Romero]]|Mushroom Gorge; 297.5, 87, -562.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during the ninth step of the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest, and asks for a [[Flower Minion]].|✕|
## Fairy Souls
There is 1 [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] found in the Mushroom Gorge:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|MD-11|254|70|-493|Jump on the cliff from 253, 73, -473 ([[Coordinates]]), the entrance to the Fairy Soul room is located at 263, 71, -488 ([[Coordinates]])|✔|

# Oasis
Categories: Locations
[Oasis]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Farming V (5) ([[Farming]])
Fairy Souls: 2
Music: None
Location: [[Mushroom Desert]]
[Inhabitants]

[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Aquamarine Dye]] [[Enchanted Cooked Mutton]] [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] [[Enchanted Raw Rabbit]] [[Lily Pad]] [[Rabbit Hide]] [[Rabbit's Foot]] [[Raw Mutton]] [[Raw Rabbit]] White Wool ([[Wool]]) [[Wool]]
Resources: [[Sand]] [[Sugar Cane]] [[Cocoa Beans]]
  Not to be confused with [[Chocolate Rabbits|Oasis]], a Beach Faction Rabbit.
The Oasis is a large area of the [[Mushroom Desert]]. It serves as a place to find [[Rabbit|Rabbits]] and [[Sheep]], harvest [[Cocoa Beans]], and go [[Fishing]] for [[Oasis Sheep]] and [[Oasis Rabbit|Oasis Rabbits]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Oasis, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Oasis
■ Fish in the pond.
## Location
The Oasis features multiple jungle trees and logs that [[Cocoa Beans]] grow on. There is a lake which is surrounded by [[Sugar Cane]] which is obtainable by the players. There is very little [[Sand]] and no [[Cactus|Cacti]] in the area. [[Rabbit|Rabbits]] and [[Sheep]] naturally spawn in the area, and [[Oasis Sheep]] and [[Oasis Rabbit|Oasis Rabbits]] can be [[Fishing|fished]] up there.

|MD-4|150|60|-448|In the secret room behind the waterfall|✔|

# Obsidian Sanctuary
Categories: Locations, Mining, Deep Caverns, Ores
[Obsidian Sanctuary]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 3
Location: [[Deep Caverns]]
← Previous Location: [[Diamond Reserve]]
Next Location →: [[Dwarven Mines]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Lift Operator]] [[Rhys]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Miner Zombie]] [[Miner Skeleton]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Bone]] [[Bone Dye]] [[Exp Share Core]] Miner Armor Piece ([[Miner Armor]]) [[Rotten Flesh]]
Resources: [[Cobblestone]] [[Diamond]] [[Block of Diamond]] [[Obsidian]]
[Metadata]
ID: DEEP_CAVERNS_ROOM_6
The Obsidian Sanctuary is the sixth floor of the [[Deep Caverns]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Obsidian Sanctuary, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Obsidian Sanctuary
■ The Obsidian Sanctuary can now be traveled to via the Lift
■ Mine Obsidian and Diamond Ore.
■ Talk to Rhys.
■ The Miner Zombies and Skeletons here are even more
## Features
The Obsidian Sanctuary features a few attractions.
### Lift Operator
In front of the lift shaft is the [[Lift Operator]], who allows players to teleport between layers of the [[Deep Caverns]] with ease. Once the player has entered the Obsidian Sanctuary and talked to the [[Lift Operator]], they will be able to teleport to the floor from any other level of the island.
### Rhys
Located at the entrance to the Obsidian Sanctuary, opposite the [[Lift Operator]] is [[Rhys]], an [[NPC]] who grants the player access to the [[Dwarven Mines]]. [[Rhys]] asks the player to bring them 15x of any enchanted ore, in exchange for access to the [[Dwarven Mines]] at the [[Lift Operator]] and a [[Heart of the Mountain]].
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Obsidian Sanctuary:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lift Operator]]|[[Lift Operator]]|Obsidian Sanctuary; 45.5, 13, 15.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to teleport to other parts of the [[Deep Caverns]], or the [[Dwarven Mines]].|✕|
|[[Rhys]]|[[Rhys]]|Obsidian Sanctuary; 31.5, 12, 14.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Unlocks access to the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Heart of the Mountain]], after the player has reached Mining XV (15) ([[Mining]]) and brought 15 enchanted ore items.|✕|
## Fairy Souls

|DC-20|3|14|51|Inside rib cage|✔|
|DC-21|-76|13|24|In the Lava|✔|
## Trivia
- During Spooky Festival, all mobs in the Obsidian Sanctuary are replaced by [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]].

# Odger's Hut
Categories: Locations, Crimson Isle
[Odger's Hut]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])
Music: "Always Nether ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Coordinates: -373 206 -802
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Odger]]
Odger's Hut is a Sub-[[Locations|Location]] inside the [[Crimson Isle]] that houses the NPC [[Odger]]. Odger's Hut sits near the top of the [[Blazing Volcano]]. During an eruption, it can still be accessed via a small back door from outside the volcano.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Odger's Hut, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Odger's Hut
■ Try to communicate with Odger.
## Appearance
A very small hut with obsidian and nether brick exterior with a black wool interior. The interior is lined with redstone lamps with a single window peeking out into some rocks.
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in Odger's Hut:

# Otherside
Categories: Locations
[Otherside]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Rift Dimension]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Unbound Explorer]]
[Metadata]
ID: RIFT
The Otherside is a sub-location of the [[Rift Dimension]]. It covers any area that is not part of another [[Locations|Zone]].
## Features
The Otherside features a few attractions.
### Lake
The Otherside is full of water and is difficult to cross by just swimming. The player can place [[Lil' Pad|Lil' Pads]] on the water to create a path that they can walk or jump across with ease. If the player stays underwater for too long, they will begin to drown and their [[Rift Time]] will decrease. Their [[Hearts]], however, will not.
There is a large rock located in the water west of the [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]], but this rock serves no purpose other than to hide the roof of the [[Mirrorverse]]'s reward room.
### Unbound Explorer
The [[Unbound Explorer]] is an extremely isolated [[NPC]] in the Otherside. They are located on a rock at the edge of the water, at the end of the Otherside furthest from the [[Wyld Woods]]. Since there is little to do in the Otherside, the player can talk to the [[Unbound Explorer]] to get teleported back to the [[Wyld Woods]] at no cost.
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Otherside:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Unbound Explorer]]|[[Unbound Explorer]]|Otherside; -28.5, 72, 315.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to teleport back to The Bastion ([[The Bastion (Rift)]]).|✕|
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Otherside.|

The entrance of the Oubliette is closed off, and the area in front of it is filled with [[Oubliette Guard|Oubliette Guards]]. Once all guards have been killed, the entrance will open, and the player can leave. Once the door opens, a message will appear in chat, saying:

The Overgrown Mushroom Cave is a sub-location in the [[Mushroom Desert]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Overgrown Mushroom Cave, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Overgrown Mushroom Cave
■ Farm mushrooms.
## Location
The area is really similar to the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]], which is connected to it, and contains one small water pool. The location has a mossy feel and has a lot of Jungle Leaves around the place. Players can find [[Mushroom]] trees, [[Mushroom|Mushrooms]] and [[Mushroom Cow|Mooshrooms]] in the area. It can be used to access the [[Mushroom Gorge]] or the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]].

# Photon Pathway
Categories: Locations
[Photon Pathway]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Rift Dimension]]
Coordinates: 57 70 -93
← Previous Location: [[Barrier Street]]
Next Location →: [[Stillgore Château]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Deer]] [[Phaser]]
[Metadata]
ID: PHOTON_PATH
The Photon Pathway is a location in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
In order to reach the [[Stillgore Château]], the player must parkour across floating islands with lasers sweeping across them. The parkour can be skipped by paying [[Phaser]] 150,000 [[Motes]]. Once the player reaches the end of the parkour, [[Phaser]] will transport them to the end for free.
[[Deer]] lies on the island at the end of the parkour. If the player has a [[Rift Boat]] in their inventory, they can talk to [[Deer]] to be transported to the [[Stillgore Château]].
Located inside a building, the player can find the ninth [[Montezuma Pet|Montezuma Soul Piece]] at [[Coordinates|152, 80, -14]]. Finding the soul piece will add an additional +15 [[Rift Time]] to the [[Montezuma Pet|Pet]].
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Photon Pathway:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Deer]]|[[Deer]]|Photon Pathway; 139, 70, -12 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to travel to [[Stillgore Château]] using a [[Rift Boat]].|✕|
|[[Phaser]]|[[Phaser]]|Photon Pathway; 60, 70, -88.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to skip to the other end of the Photon Pathway.|✕|
## Trivia
- The Photon Pathway is the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] equivalent to a river isolating the [[Stillgore Château]] from the rest of the Island. Speaking to Deer with the [[Rift Boat]] sends the player across the river triggering water particles along the players trajectory and playing splashing sounds.
  - In traditional vampire folklore vampires are unable to cross rivers. Famously Count Draculacould only cross running water at low or high tide. He is unable to fly across a river in the form of a bat or mist nor even by himself aboard a boat unless he is physically carried.
  - The Rift Guide states that "A cult of vampires has settled in the castle. Beware being sent to their prison.", however the existence of the river implies they are unable to cross to the mainland.

# Private Island
Aliases: Island size, Player's Island, Player’s Island, Private island
Categories: Locations, More info needed, Pages with UIs
[Private Island]
The initial state of the Private island
Type: Private location
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Jerry]] [[Sam]] [[Calvin]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Zombie]] [[Skeleton]] [[Spider]] [[Creeper]] Enderman ([[Enderman (Private Island)]]) [[Witch]] [[Cow]] [[Pig]] [[Chicken]] [[Sheep]] Bat ([[Bat (disambiguation)]])
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Bone]] [[Bone Dye]] [[Carrot]] [[Egg]] [[Ender Pearl]] [[Feather]] [[Glass Bottle]] [[Glowstone Dust]] [[Gunpowder]] [[Leather]] [[Poisonous Potato]] [[Potato]] [[Raw Beef]] [[Raw Chicken]] [[Raw Mutton]] [[Raw Porkchop]] [[Rotten Flesh]] [[Spider Eye]] [[String]] White Wool ([[Wool]])
The Private Island (also known as the Player's Island and Your Island) is where all players begin their SkyBlock adventure. Players can build and break blocks freely here, allowing them to customize the island to their liking. They can also place [[Minions]] to harvest resources. Except on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] mode, players can also place [[Portals]] to quickly travel to some [[Locations|Public Island]] locations. Many players use their island primarily for function, but some create interesting or useful islands for others to visit.
## Initial Island State
When the player first creates their profile, they spawn on one of two floating islands in the Private Island. The first island consists of some grass, dirt, and stone with an oak tree on top. Players are told to do some simple tasks before making their way to the second island.
The second of the two islands contains the Hub Island Portal, [[Jerry]], a [[Cobblestone Minion]], and a small cave with a torch and a chest inside. The chest contains a [[Lava Bucket]], a [[Water Bucket]], 3 [[Bone Meal]], 5 [[Dirt]], 7 [[Grass]] and 8 [[Cobblestone]]. Presumably, the [[Lava Bucket]] and [[Water Bucket]] are for a cobblestone generator, which is particularly useful on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] mode.
## Progression
Except on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] mode, after finishing the tasks for the first quest, the player is instructed to enter the portal on the second island to travel to the [[Hub Island]]. After entering the Hub Island portal for the first time, players are able to complete quests throughout the Hub and other [[Locations]]. These locations allow the player to, among other things, level up [[Skills]] and [[Collections]].
On [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] mode, the player is restricted to the Private Island and [[The Garden]] for the entirety of the game. The player will obtain items and progress without accessing public islands. There are several changes to make parts of the game's content obtainable.
## Mobs
After the first full night, mobs spawn naturally on the Private Island in areas where the light level is low enough. Mob spawning can be prevented in multiple ways:
- Using a [[Repelling Candle]] prevents all mob spawns on the island.
- Placing [[Torch|torches]] or other light sources prevents monsters from spawning in areas with enough light.
- Using a [[Day Saver]] and [[Weather Node]] on the island makes it permanently day and sunny, which makes the surface of the island constantly illuminated by the sun, so hostile mobs can't spawn.
[Lv] of the spawned mobs (except [[Creeper]]) depends on the player's [[Combat]]:
| Combat Level ([[Combat]])|Mob Level| [[Zombie]] and [[Skeleton]] armor| [[Zombie]] weapon|
|-|-|-|-|
|Combat 0 ([[Combat]])|[Lv1]|||

## Visiting
Players can visit other players' Private Islands using the command /visit <player> or through [[Christopher]]. Visiting other players' islands grants [[Social]] XP.
The maximum number of visitors on the player's own Private Island can be increased using the Guests Limit [[Account & Profile Upgrades|Profile Upgrade]]. The first upgrade is free, but subsequent upgrades require [[SkyBlock Gems]]. The Guests Limit before upgrades depends on the highest rank among the owners of the island, as shown in the table below.
|Rank|Guests Limit|
|-|-|
|1Default ([[Ranks]])|1|
|2[VIP] ([[Ranks]]) and 3[VIP+] ([[Ranks]])|3|
|4[MVP] ([[Ranks]]) and 5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]])|5|
|6[MVP++] ([[Ranks]])|7|
|7[YOUTUBE] ([[Ranks]])|15|
## Managing
### Spawn Location
Players can set their own spawn position by sleeping in a bed, or by using /spawnlocation, /setspawn, or /sethome. This location is separate from the location where Guests will spawn when they visit the island, which can be set using /guestlocation.
### Kicking
When there are one or more Guests on a player's Private Island, the owner can use either /sbkickall to kick all Guests from their island or /sbkick <player> to kick a specific player. Kicking a player also temporarily bans them from the island.
### Island Settings
The player can change settings for their Banking API and island visitor restrictions in the Island Settings menu, which can be accessed from the Settings page of the [[SkyBlock Menu]]. The player can allow visits from any combination of three options: Anyone, Friends, and Guild. The interface for the Island Settings menu is shown below.
### Island Management
Like the Island Settings menu, the Island Management menu can be accessed from the Settings page of the [[SkyBlock Menu]]. In the Island Management menu, players can manage their island's categories, name, and ranks. The interface is shown below.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Restrictions
### Island Size
The default island size is 161 blocks by 161 blocks, but it can be increased with the Island Size [[Account & Profile Upgrades|Profile Upgrade]]. Attempting to build outside these limits results in the message You can't build any further in this direction!. If the player travels vertically above Y:296, they are teleported back to their spawn location and receive the message Careful! You cannot travel too far away from the island!.
### Redstone Restrictions
Since version 0.5, redstone has been allowed on Player Islands, with strict limitations. Each island has a [[Redstone Meter]] that appears on the scoreboard when a redstone component, such as a lever, is activated. When the meter reaches 100%, redstone on the island is disabled for 10 seconds. Pistons can be placed, but cannot be activated. Minecarts are disabled due to performance issues and duplication exploits.
### Placeable Blocks Limit
Certain placeable blocks are limited to 1,000. Known blocks with this limit include [[Chest|Chests]] and [[Hopper|Hoppers]].
Blocks placed before the limit was introduced were not affected. If a player tries to place a block with it exceeding the limit, a message will show:
You have the maximum number of this block allowed on your island. (#/1000)
Certain special blocks have their own amount of placeable limit:
- [[Beacon]] - Maximum of 1
- [[Egg Hunt]] - Maximum of 10
- [[Teleport Pad]] - Maximum of 56
### Non-block Placeable Limit
The player can place [[Furniture]] in their island up to 15.
Some special furniture and entities have their own limit set and are excluded from the main limit:
- [[Century Cakes]] - Maximum of 16
- [[Experimentation Table]] - Maximum of 1
- [[Floating Crystals]] - Maximum of 1 for each type
- [[Great Spook Tree]] - Maximum of 1
- [[Island NPC]] - Maximum of 21
- [[Placeable Fairy Soul]] - Maximum of 1
- [[Postcard]] - Maximum of 1
- [[Showcase Block]] - Maximum of 10
- [[Social Display]] - Maximum of 1
- [[Training Dummy]] - Maximum of 4
- [[Farming Contest Display]] - Maximum of 1
- [[Bingo Display]] - Maximum of 1
## Trivia
- If the player falls into the void, they do not lose any [[Coins]] unless they are in [[Combat]], Death from any other source of damage such as fall damage or suffocation causes the player to lose [[Coins]].
- All fortune stats except for [[Hunting Fortune]], as well as [[Mining Spread]] (except on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]]), and [[Sweep]] are disabled on Private Islands.
  - If the player breaks a mature crop, the following message will show to remind them: STAT ALERT! Farming Fortune doesn't work on your Private Island! Try farming in Public Islands or The Garden instead! [I GET IT]
- There is a limit of 64 simultaneous primed [[TNT]] entities on the player's Private Island and [[The Garden]].
  - This limit was added because of 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] SomethingLT.

# Pumpgrotto
Categories: Locations
[Pumpgrotto]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Coordinates: -125 51 131
[Metadata]
ID: WYLD_PUMPGROTTO
The Pumpgrotto is a sub-location of the [[Wyld Woods]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Wilderness|Fairy Pond]].
## Purpose
By jumping from a great height into the water spring in the [[Wyld Woods]], instead of being launched straight back up, the player will pass through the water and down into a small cave, the Pumpgrotto, containing one [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] and nothing else. The player can then swim up the water leaking into the cave to exit the area.

The Rabbit House is a [[Locations|Location]] on the outskirts of the [[Village]] on the [[Hub Island]]. It is home to the different employees of the [[Chocolate Factory]], as well as [[Hoppity]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Rabbit House, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Rabbit House
## Purpose
The Rabbit House is a place where players can find the different employees from the [[Chocolate Factory]]. On the bottom floor of the house, seven different [[Finance Rabbit|Finance Rabbits]] and [[Rabbit Security]] can be found.
During [[Hoppity's Hunt]], [[Hoppity]] can be found outside the house, who sells different [[Chocolate Rabbits]].

# Reflective Pond
Categories: Locations, Events, Season of Jerry
[Reflective Pond]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
[Resources]
Resources: [[Ice]]
[Metadata]
ID: REFLECTIVE_POND
The Reflective Pond is a [[Locations|Location]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Reflective Pond, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Reflective Pond
■ Reflect upon the damage you have caused this land.
■ Contemplate what lies beneath.
## Features
### Ice
The Reflective Pond's surface naturally turns into [[Ice]], which can be broken. Breaking the ice reveals the water beneath, in which the player can fish up a variety of [[Sea Creatures|Winter Sea Creatures]]. Once the ice on the surface is broken, it will regenerate after a while and must be broken again to reach the water.

[Regalia Room]

The Regalia Room is a [[Locations|Location]] under the [[Auction House]], where certain items can be bought. It can only be accessed if the player has [[Shen's Regalia]].
## Purpose
There are 3 items located in the Regalia Room, which can be purchased for [[Coins]]. The first two items are always present, while the third one rotates every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]]. The third item is the same for all players.
Players can only purchase 4 [[Enchanted Book|Divine Gift Books]] and 4 [[The Art of Peace|Arts of Peace]] per day, the daily limit resets at midnight [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] Eastern Time. There is no limit on the special item.

# Rift Gallery
Categories: Locations
[Rift Gallery]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Rift Dimension]]

[Inhabitants]

[Metadata]
ID: RIFT_GALLERY
The Rift Gallery is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Museum]].
## Features
The Rift Gallery features multiple attractions. The Rift Gallery can be accessed using the [[Teleport Pad]] in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]] after the player bring the [[Supreme Timecharm]] to [[Elise (Rift)|Elise]].
### Securing Timecharms
The player can secure [[Rift Timecharms]] by placing them in their corresponding slots in the Rift Gallery. This unlocks new [[Locations|Zones]] in the Rift and grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+100 SkyBlock XP]] from the [[SkyBlock Levels|"Timecharms" SkyBlock Levels Task]].
|Condition|Guaranteed Message|
|-|-|
|Interacting with an empty slot|Secure the timecharm belonging to this slot after you find it!|
|Interacting with an empty slot|This is where I'd put my timecharm...IF I HAD ONE!!!!!!!|
|Interacting with an empty slot while holding a non-matching Rift Timecharm ([[Rift Timecharms]])|Uh... that's not where this timecharm goes.|
|Interacting with a slot holding a Timecharm|This is the <Timecharm>!|
### Montezuma Cat Soul
Inside the miniature house in the [[West Village]] section of the Rift Gallery, the player can find the second [[Montezuma Pet|Montezuma Soul Piece]], which is located at [[Coordinates|0, 56, 63]]. Finding the soul piece will add an additional +15 [[Rift Time]] to the [[Montezuma Pet|Pet]].
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Rift Gallery:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Elise (Rift)]]|[[Elise (Rift)]]|Rift Gallery; 9.875, 55, 76 ([[Coordinates]])|Manages the Rift Gallery.|✕|
## Trivia
- The gray quote when interacting with an empty slot is a reference to a popular quote from the TV series The Fairly OddParents.

Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Rift Dimension]]
← Previous Location: Wizard Tower ([[Wizard Tower (Rift)]])
Next Location →: [[Rift Gallery]]
[Metadata]
ID: RIFT_GALLERY_ENTRANCE
The Rift Gallery Entrance is a location in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the entrance to the [[Museum]].
## Features
The Rift Gallery Entrance features a few attractions.
### Rift Gallery
Located at the far end of the Rift Gallery Entrance is a portal, which when the player enters, will teleport them to the [[Rift Gallery]]. Here, the player can store their [[Rift Timecharms]] to unlock permanent buffs such as [[Rift Time]], access to more [[Locations|zones]], [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Experience]] and increased stats on the [[Rift Necklace]].
### Flight
As soon as the player sets foot in the Rift Gallery Entrance, they will be lifted upwards into the air and flown down the entrance into the portal to the [[Rift Gallery]]. The players route twists and turns slightly and is not direct, meaning occasionally, they can get stuck behind blocks. The direction in which the player is facing does not affect the direction in which they travel, meaning no matter what, they cannot leave the Rift Gallery Entrance once they enter it.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Rift Gallery Entrance.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Rift Gallery Entrance.|

# Royal Mines
Categories: Locations, Dwarven Mines, Mining
[Royal Mines]
Type: [[Mining]] location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]])
Fairy Souls: 1

[Inhabitants]

The Royal Mines are is a section of the [[Dwarven Mines]] located below [[Palace Bridge]], and connected to [[Cliffside Veins]], and [[Aristocrat Passage]].
## Purpose
The Royal Mines not only contains [[Mithril]], but also veins of [[Ores|Pure]] [[Coal]], [[Iron Ingot|Iron]], [[Gold Ingot|Gold]], [[Lapis Lazuli|Lapis]], [[Redstone Dust|Redstone]], [[Emerald]], and [[Diamond]].
A tunnel connects the main section to a lower part of the Royal Mines, where a [[Ticket Master]] can be found. This section also leads to the [[Great Ice Wall]].

|[[Marigold]]|[[Marigold]]|Royal Mines; 181.5, 150, 60.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells perks in exchange for [[Gold Essence]] as part of the Gold Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]).|✔|
|[[Ticket Master]]|[[Ticket Master]]|Royal Mines; 84.5, 114, 134.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to travel to various parts of the [[Dwarven Mines]] in exchange for [[Coins]].|✕|

The Royal Palace is a castle in [[Dwarven Mines]], where players can find the [[King|Dwarven Kings]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Royal Palace, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Royal Palace
■ Long live the King(s)!
■ Talk to the Puzzler.
## Overview
The Royal Palace is home to the [[King|Kings]] and [[Queen Mismyla]], as well as several [[Castle Guards]]. Here, players can access their [[Commissions]]. The [[Raffle]] event can take place here.
The Royal Palace branches into the [[Grand Library]], [[Royal Quarters]], [[Barracks of Heroes]], and [[Hanging Court]].

The Ruins (also known as Castle) is a location on the [[Hub Island]], which is home to aggressive [[Wolf|Wolves]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Ruins, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Ruins
■ Explore the ancient ruins.
■ Watch out for the guard dogs!
## Functions
The Ruins is found to the left of the launch pad to [[The Park]].
It contains [[Wolf|Wolves]] and [[Old Wolf|Old Wolves]], and is one of the locations at which the [[Sven Packmaster]] is spawned to complete [[Wolf Slayer]] quests, though the [[Howling Cave]] may be a better choice, because the Wolves there grant more [[Combat|Combat XP]].
The [[Lonely Philosopher]] is also located here, who sells [[Travel Scroll to Hub Castle]].

# Savanna Woodland
Categories: Locations
[Savanna Woodland]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Sub-locations: [[Soul Cave]]
Fairy Souls: 2
Music: "Sky of Trees ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[The Park]]
Requirements: The Campfire Cult ([[Quests]]) Quest
← Previous Location: [[Dark Thicket]]
Next Location →: [[Jungle Island]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Master Tactician Funk]] [[Romero]] [[Worker Xavier]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Acacia Log]] Yellow Rock ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]]) [[Melody's Shoes]] [[Tactician's Sword]] [[Wood Singularity]]
The Savanna Woodland is a sub-location of [[The Park]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Savanna Woodland, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Savanna Woodland
■ Chop down Acacia logs.
## Features
The Savanna Woodland features various attractions.
### Melody's Harp
[[Melody]] is an [[NPC]] located within [[Melody's Plateau]], which is connected directly to the Savanna Woodlands. She is part of "The Rebuild" [[Quests|Quest]], and requires the player to bring her 512 (8 stacks) [[Acacia Log|Acacia Logs]] to repair her Harp. The player will be rewarded for completing the quest with access to the Harp, [[Melody's Shoes]], 2,000 [[Coins]], +1,500 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]], [[SkyBlock Levels|+5 SkyBlock XP]], and access to the [[Jungle Island]].
### Master Tactician Funk
Located in a tent near the edge of the Savanna Woodland is the [[Master Tactician Funk]], who sells the [[Tactician's Sword]] and [[Wood Singularity]].
### Gold Mine
Located near the center of the Savanna Woodland is a tiny gold mine which can be mined for [[Ores|Gold Ore]]. When [[Romero]] asks the player to get him [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|gold]] from this gold mine, the gold has a chance of dropping a [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|Yellow Rock]] instead of the ore.
### Romero
[[Romero]] appears in a cave below the Savanna Woodland. He begins the quest there, and requests a [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|Yellow Rock]] before progressing further. After doing so, he will move between locations all across [[SkyBlock]], until he is reunited with [[Juliette]] on the [[Jungle Island]].
### Soul Cave
Near the middle of the gold mine in the Savanna Woodland lies a breakable wall that can be blown up with an [[Explosive Bow]] or [[Superboom TNT]]. Here, you will find the [[Soul Cave]], where [[Pack Spirit|Pack Spirits]], [[Howling Spirit|Howling Spirits]], and [[Soul of the Alpha|Soul of the Alphas]] will spawn. This is one of three caves designed for [[Wolf Slayer]].
## NPCs

|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Master Tactician Funk]]|[[Master Tactician Funk]]|Savanna Woodland; -452.5, 110, 29.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells the [[Tactician's Sword]] and [[Wood Singularity]].|✔|
|[[Romero]]|[[Romero]]|Savanna Woodland; -456.5, 100, 30.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during the first step of the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest, and asks the Yellow Rock ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]]).|✕|
|[[Worker Xavier]]|[[Worker Xavier]]|Savanna Woodland; -424.5, 110, -15.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Tasks the player with checking on [[Melody]].|✕|

# Security Hall

The Security Hall is an [[Locations|Location]] found in the [[Community Center]]. Players above [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 10]] can access the Security Hall after talking to [[Susan]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Security Hall, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Security Hall
## Overview
The Security Hall serves as a place where players can learn about account safety.
Players cannot send or receive chat messages while inside the Security Hall. Attempting to chat provides the message:
The chat is silenced while you're in the Security Hall!
Exit the Security Hall to resume chatting.

# Shen's Auction
Aliases: Shen Auction, Shen auction, Shen's Auctions, Shen's Special, Shens Special
Categories: Locations
[Shen's Auction]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Coordinates: -21 55 -10
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Damia]]
Shen's Auction is a [[Locations|Location]] under the [[Auction House]], where certain items are auctioned.
## Mechanics
Every SkyBlock Year, a new auction will start. Players can bid on items that are being auctioned on Shen's Auction and Shen's Special. The top bidders on items can claim the items after the auction has ended. Not all items will always appear.
- [[Diaz]] elected with the Volume Trading perk doubles the quantity of all items available to buy on both Shen's Auction and Shen's Special.
- [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] players have 50% of the normal stock available. Additionally, [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] players bid separately from Classic profiles. All items claimed from Ironman Shen's Auction are automatically [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]].
- [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] players are blocked from this feature.
- [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] players do not have access to this feature.
### Shen's Auction
If the player has reached [[Museum|Museum Milestone 10]], they can access Shen's Auction. Shen's Auction always auctions the same items.
Auctioned Items (Shen's Auction):
|Slot|Name|No. of winning players Classic|No. of winning players ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]])|No. of winning players Classic with [[Diaz]]|No. of winning players ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]]) with [[Diaz]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Artifact of Control]]|[[Artifact of Control]]|20|10|40|20|
|[[Pandora's Box]]|[[Pandora's Box]]|40|20|80|40|
|[[Shen's Regalia]]|[[Shen's Regalia]]|80|40|160|80|
Attempting to view Shen's Auction items before talking to [[Damia]] will give the following message: I suggest talking to Damia before trying to access this!
### Shen's Special
If the player has [[Museum|Museum Milestone 20]], they can access Shen's Special. These items always appear in the same pairs and run in a specific order.
|Slot|Name|No. of winning players Classic|No. of winning players ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]])|No. of winning players Classic with [[Diaz]]|No. of winning players ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]]) with [[Diaz]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Priceless the Fish]]|[[Priceless the Fish]]|5|2|10|5|
|[[Giant Fishing Rod]]|[[Giant Fishing Rod]]|10|5|20|10|
|[[Clover Helmet]]|[[Clover Helmet]]|10|5|20|10|
|[[Racing Helmet]]|[[Racing Helmet]]|10|5|20|10|
|[[Dirt Rod]]|[[Dirt Rod]]|15|7|30|15|
|[[Wizard Wand]]|[[Wizard Wand]]|20|10|40|20|
While [[Diaz|Mayor Diaz]] is elected with her Volume Trading perk active, two additional items are added, chosen from the following list:
|Slot|Name|No. of winning players Classic|No. of winning players ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hoe of No Tilling]]|[[Hoe of No Tilling]]|20|10|
|[[Knuckle Sandwich]]|[[Knuckle Sandwich]]|20|10|
|[[Puzzle Cube]]|[[Puzzle Cube]]|20|10|
|[[Shimmersparkle Chestplate]]|[[Shimmersparkle Chestplate]]|20|10|
|[[Spelling Stick]]|[[Spelling Stick]]|20|10|
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in Shen's Auction:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Damia]]|[[Damia]]|Shen's Auction; -16.5, 55, -10.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to Shen's Auction and Shen.|✕|
## Fairy Souls
There is 1 [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] found in Shen's Auction:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|4|-39|56|-11|Inside the fireplace|✔|
## Trivia
- The [[Estranged Taikan Mask]] was expected to be in the Shen's Special rotation when [[Diaz]] is elected, but it has never appeared. Eventually, the developers shared that the item would be too polarizing given its ability.

|Oct 10, 2025|0.23.5 RC|Added separate Shen's Auction for ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]]) players.|

# Shifted Tavern
Categories: Locations
[Shifted Tavern]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Coordinates: -126 73 171
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Alabaster]] [[Arora]] [[Ashera]] [[Chester]] [[Dackinoru]] [[Fafnir]] [[Garlacius]] [[Lazarus]] [[Seskel]] [[Shifted]] [[Tybalt]] [[Vreike]]
[Metadata]
ID: SHIFTED_TAVERN
The Shifted Tavern is a sub-location of the [[Wyld Woods]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Wilderness|Tavern]].
## Features
The Shifted Tavern features a few attractions.
### Customers
The Shifted Tavern is full of tables, with various [[NPC|NPCs]] sitting at them. The player cannot talk to them, and the NPCs will not grant dialogue unless having purchased a tip to obtain the [[Stability Elixir]]. The NPCs converse with one another through holograms. The paid tips lead from [[Shifted]] to a bunch of other NPCs and then to [[Fafnir]], who is the last NPC of the chain. After speaking to [[Fafnir]], the player is able to obtain a [[Stability Elixir]] from the [[Argofay Serialbather]]. The [[Argofay Serialbather]] will not be of any use unless the player finishes speaking with all the NPCs.
### Shifted
[[Shifted]] is an [[NPC]] and the main feature of the Shifted Tavern. They are a reference to [[Shifty]], and own the Shifted Tavern. The player can interact with them to open their shop, where they can purchase various elixirs for [[Motes]]. [[Shifted]] will also converse with [[Dackinoru]] through holograms.
## NPCs

# Slimehill
Aliases: Slime Hill
Categories: Locations, Mining, Deep Caverns
[Slimehill]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 4
Location: [[Deep Caverns]]
← Previous Location: [[Pigmen's Den]]
Next Location →: [[Diamond Reserve]]

[Metadata]
ID: DEEP_CAVERNS_ROOM_4
Slimehill is the fourth floor of the [[Deep Caverns]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Slimehill, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Slimehill
■ The Slimehill can now be traveled to via the Lift Operator.
■ Mine Emerald ore.
■ This area is covered with Emerald Slimes!
## Features
Slimehill features a few attractions.
### Lift Operator
In front of the lift shaft is the [[Lift Operator]], who allows players to teleport between layers of the [[Deep Caverns]] with ease. Once the player has entered Slimehill and talked to the [[Lift Operator]], they will be able to teleport to the floor from any other level of the island.
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Slimehill:

The Smoldering Tomb is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Crimson Isle]]. It has a recommended [[Combat]] level of 30.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Smoldering Tomb, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Smoldering Tomb
■ Slay really mean blazes.
## Features
### Mobs
The Smoldering Tomb is meant be used to obtain [[Combat|Combat XP]] for [[Blaze Slayer]]. [[Smoldering Blaze|Smoldering Blazes]] spawn throughout the Tomb, and [[Millennia-Aged Blaze|Millennia-Aged Blazes]] spawn in the lower areas.
### Resources
[[Gemstone|Opal Gemstones]] are generated naturally in the Smoldering Tomb.
[[Cinderbat Shard|Cinderbat Shards]] can be caught by using a [[Huntraps|huntrap]].

# South Reaches
Categories: Locations
[South Reaches]
Type: [[Foraging]] location
Level: Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]])
Music: "Moonglade Marsh ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Moonglade Marsh]]
Coordinates: -690 120 70
[Inhabitants]

[Resources]
Resources: [[Fig Log]] [[Lushlilac]]
[Metadata]
ID: SOUTH_REACHES
The South Reaches are a sub-location of the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Features
The South Reaches are a series of large plateaus connected by bridges with [[Fig Log|Fig Trees]] to chop. Additionally, [[Lushlilac|Lushlilac Bushes]] can be picked for [[Lushlilac]].
### Moonglade Beacon
[[Boris]] introduces the player to the [[Moonglade Beacon]], which is an island-wide effect that grants all players [[Fig Fortune]] and [[Mangrove Fortune]] depending on its current stack and signal strength. Successfully tuning the color, speed, and pitch of the frequency grants +1 stack to all players and lasts for 20 minutes.
The signal strength can be increased with a [[Signal Enhancer]], which can be obtained from [[Tree Gifts]].
### Trial of Blue Flames
[[Brian]] is the leader of the Soul Campfire Cult. Unlike the [[Trials of Fire|Trial of Fire]], the [[Trial of Blue Flames]] calculates [[Defense]]. Completing a trial upgrades the [[Soul Campfire Badge]].
### Sawyer
[[Sawyer]] introduces the player to [[Boosters]] and sells a [[Common Foraging Fortune Booster|Foraging Fortune Booster]].
## NPCs
There are 5 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the South Reaches:

|[[Brian]]|[[Brian]]|South Reaches; -628.5, 117, 47.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to the [[Trial of Blue Flames]].|✕|
|[[Sawyer]]|[[Sawyer]]|South Reaches; -691.5, 121, 81.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to [[Boosters]] and sells the Foraging Fortune Booster ([[Common Foraging Fortune Booster]]).|✔|
|[[Soul Campfire Adept]]|[[Soul Campfire Adept]]|South Reaches; -624.5, 117, 51.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about [[Brian]]'s campfire cult.|✕|
|[[Soul Campfire Initiate]]|[[Soul Campfire Initiate]]|South Reaches; -624.5, 117, 47.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about [[Brian]]'s campfire cult.|✕|
|[[Soul Campfire Initiate]]|[[Soul Campfire Initiate]]|South Reaches; -628.5, 117, 51.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about [[Brian]]'s campfire cult.|✕|

# Spider's Den

Next Location →: [[Crimson Isle]] [[The End]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Archaeologist]] [[Bramass Beastslayer]] [[Grandma Wolf]] [[Haymitch]] [[Ike]] [[Michael]] [[Rick]] [[Shaggy]] [[Spider Tamer]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Arachne's Brood]] [[Arachne]] [[Arachne's Keeper]] [[Broodmother]] [[Cave Spider (Broodmother)]] [[Dasher Spider]] [[Flint Skeleton]] [[Rain Slime]] [[Toxic Rain Slime]] [[Splitter Spider]] [[Silverfish (Splitter Spider)]] [[Spider Jockey]] [[Jockey Skeleton]] [[Silverfish (Jockey Skeleton)]] [[Voracious Spider]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Arachne's Belt]] [[Arachne's Calling]] [[Arachne's Cloak]] [[Arachne's Gloves]] [[Arachne's Necklace]] [[Bone]] [[Bone Dye]] [[Enchanted Bone]] [[Enchanted Flint]] [[Enchanted Slimeball]] [[Flint]] [[Luxurious Spool]] [[Poison Sample]] [[Slimeball]] [[Spider Eye]] [[Spider Talisman]] [[String]] [[Tarantula Pet]] [[Travel Scroll to Spider's Den Top of Nest]]
Resources: [[Gravel]] [[Flint]]
The Spider's Den is a [[Combat]] location unlocked after reaching [[Combat|Combat I (1)]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Spider's Den, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Spider's Den
■ Talk to Haymitch.
■ Watch out for spiders!
■ Try to make it to the top of the Spider Mound.
■ Learn about the Bestiary.
## Features
The Spider's Den can be accessed after the player has attained [[Combat|Combat I (1)]] by using a [[Launch Pad|Launchpad]] found in the back of the [[Graveyard]]. It can also be accessed with the [[Portals|Portal to The Spider's Den]] or [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll to the Spider's Den]]. This will take the player to a different sub-server containing The Spiders Den.
The Spider's Den has multiple sub-regions: The [[Spider Mound]] with multiple chasms and stairs containing weaker mobs, [[Grandma's House]] containing multiple [[NPC|NPCs]], the [[Gravel Mines]], and [[Arachne's Burrow]], [[Archaeologist's Camp]] with [[Archaeologist]] and [[Arachne's Sanctuary]] containing stronger versions of the spiders found in the den (This is where you can find the [[Arachne]] boss). The spiders get stronger in deeper regions of the cave. These enemies include:
- [[Dasher Spider|Dasher Spiders]], which dash towards players.
- [[Splitter Spider|Splitter Spiders]], which spawn silverfish upon death.
- [[Weaver Spider|Weaver Spiders]], which shoot cobweb at the players.
- [[Voracious Spider|Voracious Spiders]], which have significantly higher health and damage than other spiders.
- [[Spider Jockey|Spider Jockeys]], which have a [[Jockey Skeleton]] on their back.
In [[Arachne's Burrow]], all spiders have a chance to drop an [[Arachne's Calling]], which can be used to summon the boss [[Arachne]], which has two versions depending on how she's summoned.
On the outskirts of the islands, one may also find [[Rain Slime|Rain Slimes]], [[Toxic Rain Slime|Toxic Rain Slimes]] and [[Flint Skeleton|Flint Skeletons]], with the former spawning on the road between [[Arachne's Burrow]] and the Spiders Den during rain while the latter spawns near the gravel pit.
Every 40 minutes on the Spider's Den, 20 minutes of [[Weather|Rain]] begins. While raining, players gain +25 [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Rain Slime|Rain Slimes]] spawn. After raining twice, the third rain becomes a thunderstorm and in addition to previous effects [[Toxic Rain Slime|Toxic Rain Slimes]] start to spawn.
## Zones
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|Spider's Den|The first [[Combat]]-based island. Requires Combat I (1) ([[Combat]]) to access.|[[Haymitch]], [[Rick]]|
|^ [[Spider Mound]]|A large structure containing low-level spiders.|None|
|^ [[Gravel Mines]]|Contains lots of [[Gravel]] for players to mine.|None|
|^ [[Michael's Crib]]|Serves as an introduction to [[Mob Types]].|[[Ike]], [[Michael]]|
|^ [[Grandma's House]]|Allows the player to unlock [[Bestiary]], complete Shaggy's Quest ([[Quests]]), and obtain the [[Grandma Wolf Pet]].|[[Bramass Beastslayer]], [[Grandma Wolf]], [[Shaggy]]|
|^ [[Archaeologist's Camp]]|Serves as an introduction to [[Relics]], which grant 10,000 [[Coins]] each when found for the first time.|[[Archaeologist]]|
|^ [[Arachne's Burrow]]|Contains higher level spiders, often slain for [[Spider Slayer]] quests.|None|
|^ [[Arachne's Sanctuary]]|Serves as a place to summon [[Arachne]].|[[Spider Tamer]]|
### Tiny Island
The Tiny Island is a feature located in between [[The End]] and the Spiders Den. It can be accessed by a [[Barrier]] bridge. The island's purpose is to house a single [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]].
The navigation shown should be followed with your character facing North, while not changing your point of view! Hold down ⇧ Shift to avoid falling off.
Show/Hide Instructions
Visualization of the path from a top-down view
1. 4 blocks left
2. 4 blocks forward
3. 5 blocks left
4. 5 blocks backward
5. 5 blocks left
6. 3 blocks forward
7. 9 blocks left
8. 1 block backward
9. 3 blocks left
10. 3 blocks backward
11. 4 blocks left
12. 3 blocks backward
13. 3 blocks left
14. 4 blocks backward
15. 4 blocks right
16. 1 block forward
17. 1 block right
18. 1 block forward
19. 2 blocks right
20. 4 blocks backward
21. 1 block left
22. 3 blocks backward
23. 2 blocks left
Alternatively, player can navigate by looking for borders of invisible blocks that appear when block is pointed at with the crosshair.
It is also possible to use an [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Grappling Hook]] and get there although it may be quite difficult.

## Trivia
- The skeleton of the dragon is revealed to be the Chozon, one of the generals of the Great Dragon, Jythar who lies next to [[The Bastion]], on the [[Crimson Isle]].
  - [[Corm]] (on the [[Crimson Isle]]) reveals this in their [[Corm|dialogue]].
- While the Spider's Den applies the Mining Fatigue effect to the player, the [[Gravel]] is not affected by [[Mining Speed]], instead it emulates vanilla mining behavior.
