# Spring Depths
Categories: Locations
[Spring Depths]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]]); [[Heart of the Forest]] Tier 4
Sub-locations: [[Hotspot Haven]] [[Ant's Cave]] [[Desert Temple]]
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: "Torrhus Canyon ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]
Coordinates: -641 118 183
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Ant (NPC)]] [[Hunter McNeil]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Solar]] [[Water Snake]] [[Ember]] [[Sepialot]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Ember Shard]] [[Enchanted Ruby Veilshroom]] [[Ruby Veilshroom]] [[Sepialot Shard]] [[Solar Shard]] [[Water Snake Shard]]
The Spring Depths is a [[Locations|Location]] found within the [[Torrhus Springs]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Spring Depths, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Spring Depths
■ Gather more Sea Lumies.
■ Use your Fishing Net to Hunt.
■ Swim down deeper into the Spring Depths.
## Features
The Spring Depths represents the lower portion of the tall body of water that makes up the [[Torrhus Springs]]. Players can find [[Water Snake|Water Snakes]], [[Solar|Solars]], [[Ember|Embers]], and [[Sepialot|Sepialots]], all of which can be [[Hunting|hunted]] using a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]].
There are also several pathways, including one that leads to a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]], one for the [[Ant's Cave]], and one for the [[Hotspot Haven]]. At the bottom of the water, players can find a puzzle to access the [[Desert Temple]].
## Zones
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Ant's Cave]]|Contains Ant ([[Ant (NPC)]]) and Ants ([[Ant]]). Accessed from a path in the Spring Depths.|[[Ant (NPC)]]|
|[[Hotspot Haven]]|Contains a Fishing Hotspot ([[Fishing Hotspots]]) that lets players fish up the the [[Giant Water Bug Shard]]. Accessed from a path in the Spring Depths.|[[Hunter McNeil]]|
|[[Desert Temple]]|Accessed at the bottom of the Spring Depths and after completing a color puzzle.|None|
## NPCs

|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ant (NPC)]]|[[Ant (NPC)]]|[[Ant's Cave]]; -615.5, 75, 208.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells Tier XII [[Foraging]] Minion ([[Minions]]) Upgrade Stones.|✔|
|[[Hunter McNeil]]|[[Hunter McNeil]]|[[Hotspot Haven]]; -652.5, 103, 167.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the nearby Fishing Hotspot ([[Fishing Hotspots]]) that grants a chance to catch a [[Giant Water Bug Shard]].|✕|
## Fairy Souls

|No.|X|Y|Z|Location|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|TC-1|-618|125|179|Spring Depths|Accessed from a cave entrance|✔|

# Spring Path
Categories: Locations
[Spring Path]
Type: [[Foraging]] location
Level: Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]]); [[Heart of the Forest]] Tier 4
Music: "Torrhus Canyon ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"

The Spring Path is a [[Locations|Location]] found on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. It connects the bottom part of the island to the [[Torrhus Springs]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Spring Path, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Spring Path
■ Climb up to the peak of Torrhus Springs.
■ Meet Miria and partake in Miria's Contests.
■ Keep an eye out for pesky Mountain Goats!
## Features
The Spring Path serves as a connecting path to the [[Torrhus Springs]]. It starts just near [[Honey]]'s booth and [[Miria's Hut]].
On the path, players can find [[Mountain Goat|Mountain Goats]] and [[Hunter Jean]]. There are also some water pools that players can go [[Fishing]] from.

# Spruce Woods
Categories: Locations
[Spruce Woods]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: "Sky of Trees ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"

Requirements: Into the Woods ([[Quests]]) Quest
← Previous Location: [[Birch Park]]

[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Gustave]] [[Kelly]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Spruce Log]] [[Kelly's T-Shirt]]
The Spruce Woods is a sub-location of [[The Park]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Spruce Woods, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Spruce Woods
■ Chop down Spruce logs.
## Features
The Spruce Woods features a few attractions.
### Kelly
[[Kelly]] is an [[NPC]] located right in the middle of the Spruce Woods. They are part of the "A Helping Hand" [[Quests|Quest]], and require the player to chop down 128 (2 stacks) [[Spruce Log|Spruce Logs]]. The player will be rewarded for completing the quest with [[Kelly's T-Shirt]], 1,500 [[Coins]], +500 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]], and access to the [[Dark Thicket]].
### Woods Racing
The [[Woods Race]] is an ongoing quest that begins once the player talks to [[Gustave]]. The player must run over a pressure plate next to [[Gustave]] in the Spruce Woods before crossing over to the [[Dark Thicket]], followed by the [[Savanna Woodland]] and [[Jungle Island]] and run over another pressure plate, before returning to the original one to end the race. Completing races under certain amounts of time yields unique items.
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Spruce Woods:

|[[Kelly]]|[[Kelly]]|Spruce Woods; -350.5, 94, 33.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Rewards the player with [[Kelly's T-Shirt]] after bringing 128 Spruce Logs ([[Spruce Log]]).|✕|

The Squid Cave is a sub-location of the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Features
The Squid Cave can be accessed from a tunnel in the [[Murkwater Depths]]. It features several [[Lumisquid|Lumisquids]] that may be hunted.
## Tips
- Since a large amount of [[Pressure Resistance]] and [[Respiration]] is needed to reach the Squid Cave, and [[Lumisquid]] is difficult to hunt with low-level [[Fishing Nets]], it is recommended to place down [[Huntraps]] until the player's [[Pressure Resistance]] is reasonably high and they have a [[Turbo Fishing Net]].

The Stillgore Château is a location in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Tasks
Viewing the [[Rift Guide]] for the Stillgore Château will display the following tasks:
Things to do:
⇨ Craft the Crux Chronomicon
⇨ Enigma: Standing there
⇨ Enigma: Between Towers
⇨ Enigma: Castle Fleespook
⇨ Enigma: Castle Flower Pot
⇨ Enigma: Fairylosopher
⇨ Eye: The Prince
And 1 more!
Enigma Souls: 6/6
## Features
The Stillgore Château features many attractions. The player must have talked to [[Deer]] after crossing the [[Photon Pathway]] in order to access the location.
### Oubliette
If the player loses all their [[Hearts]], they will be transported to the [[Oubliette]]. The player can escape after killing all of the [[Oubliette Guard|Oubliette Guards]].
### Porhtal's Eye
[[Porhtal|The Prince]] is the eye in the Stillgore Château.
### Blood Effigies
There are 6 Blood Effigies located in the Stillgore Château. Each Blood Effigy has 3x Block of Redstone, which can be broken from top to bottom to grant everyone on the island +2 [[Rift Damage]] for 20 minutes. The Bedrock on each Blood Effigy can also be clicked to reset the timer when it is granting [[Rift Damage]].
Blood Effigies:

[Metadata]
ID: STRIDEMBER_FISSURE
The Stride-Ember Fissure is a sub-location of the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Features
The Stride-Ember Fissure is a large pool of lava with several steppingstones scattered around. [[Stridersurfer|Stridersurfers]] wander about the fissure. Additionally, they can be fished up upon reaching [[Fishing|Fishing XVII (17)]].
The Stride-Ember Fissure can be accessed through the [[Side-Ember Way]] in the [[North Wetlands]] or the [[Dive-Ember Pass]] in the [[Murkwater Depths]].

The Stronghold is a [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Crimson Isle]] and the first location on the island.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Stronghold, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Stronghold
■ Collect Nether Wart.
■ Talk to Elle.
■ Defeat Bladesoul.
## Features
The following [[Mob|Mobs]] spawn in the Stronghold:
- [[Bezal]]
- [[Blaze]]
- [[Mutated Blaze]]
- [[Wither Skeleton]]
- [[Wither Spectre]]
[[Bladesoul]] spawns on the right side of the Stronghold.
The [[Trophy Fish|Soul Fish]] can be fished up in the Stronghold.

# Sunken Jerry Pond
Categories: Locations, Events, Season of Jerry
[Sunken Jerry Pond]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Jerry's Workshop]]
Coordinates: -101 64 80
[Resources]
Resources: [[Ice]]
[Metadata]
ID: SUNKEN_ICE_POND
The Sunken Jerry Pond is a [[Locations|Location]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Sunken Jerry Pond, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Sunken Jerry Pond
■ A sunken plateau slowly falling into the void.
## Features
The Sunken Jerry Pond features one main attraction.
### Ice
The Sunken Jerry Pond's surface naturally turns into [[Ice]], which can be broken. Breaking the ice reveals the water beneath, in which the player can fish up a variety of [[Sea Creatures|Winter Sea Creatures]]. Once the ice on the surface is broken, it will regenerate after a while and must be broken again to reach the water.
## Trivia
- The Sunken Jerry Pond's surface freezes into Ice faster than the [[Jerry Pond]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 30, 2022|Added Sunken Jerry Pond.|

[Metadata]
ID: MOONGLADE_VILLAGE
Tangleburg is a [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]] in [[Galatea]].
## Purpose
Tangleburg serves as an introductory spot for [[Galatea]] and its various mechanics. Players are first instructed to speak with [[Hina]], who unlocks the [[Heart of the Forest]], a skill tree that grants various [[Foraging]]-related perks. After then speaking to [[Swoop]] and learning about [[Sweep]], players can start [[David Hunterborough]]'s quest, who explains [[Hunting]] and [[Attributes]].

Taylor's is a sub-location of the [[Village Plaza]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
### Seymour
Located on Taylor's porch is [[Seymour (Rift)|Seymour]]. They sell [[Exceedingly Comfy Sneakers]] in exchange for [[Gunther's Sneakers]] and 49,999 [[Motes]]. Purchasing the former will complete the task "Fancy Sneakers" in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift Guide]].
### Reverse-Murder Investigation
After scanning the wedding dress in the [[Murder House]] with a [[Detective Scanner]], [[Detective Amog]] will appear in Taylor's basement, next to [[Joliet]] and [[Taylor (Rift)|Rift]]. After talking to [[Joliet]], the player must go back to the [[Murder House]] in order to progress in the quest.

Taylor's Shop is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Village]] where [[Taylor]] resides, who sells cosmetics for [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Taylor's Shop, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Taylor's Shop
■ Purchase various cosmetics for your Pets, Helmets, and more!
## Purpose
Taylor's Shop is home to [[Taylor]], who sells cosmetics for [[SkyBlock Gems]]. There is also a basement, where the [[Fashion Shop]] and [[Seymour]] is located.

Terry's Shack is a [[Locations|Location]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Terry's Shack, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Terry's Shack
■ Talk to Terry.
■ Browse Terry's unique wares.
## Purpose
[[Terry]] is an [[NPC]] located inside Terry's Shack. They sell 4 different [[Reforge Stones]]: [[Salt Cube]], [[Rusty Anchor]], [[Terry's Snowglobe]] and [[Frozen Bauble]]. Previously, they used to apply the [[Reforging|Salty]] and [[Reforging|Treacherous]] reforges onto [[Fishing Rods]] at a cost.

# Tewtil Tunnel
Categories: Locations
[Tewtil Tunnel]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])

The Tewtil Tunnel is a [[Fishing]] [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]] that contains [[Tewtil|Tewtils]].
## Features
The Tewtil Tunnel primarily serves as a place for [[Tewtil|Tewtils]] to spawn, which can be fed [[Trophy Frogs]] to be exploded, granting the [[Tewtil Shard]]. It also features [[Lotum|Lotums]], which can be punched to obtain the [[Lotum Shard]], as well as the [[Lotus Atoll|Lily Pads]], which can be merged together to obtain the [[Trophy Frogs|Exploding Frog]] [[Trophy Frogs|Trophy Frog]].
## NPCs

|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Researcher Jessica]]|[[Researcher Jessica]]|Tewtil Tunnel; 75.5, 64, -10.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Hints about how to obtain Tewtil Shards ([[Tewtil Shard]]).|✕|
## Trivia
- [[Tewtil|Tewtils]] can spawn all over the [[Lotus Atoll]], not just in this tunnel.

# The Barn
Aliases: Barn
Categories: Locations
[The Barn]
Type: [[Farming]] location

Level: Farming I (1) ([[Farming]])

[Inhabitants]

Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Cow]] [[Pig]] [[Chicken]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Egg]] [[Feather]] [[Leather]] [[Raw Beef]] [[Raw Chicken]] [[Raw Porkchop]]
Resources: [[Wheat]] [[Potato]] [[Carrot]] [[Milk Bucket]] [[Melon]] [[Pumpkin]]
The Barn is an island in [[The Farming Islands (disambiguation)|The Farming Islands]] featuring several [[Crops (disambiguation)|crops]] that are not obtainable at the [[Farm]] on the [[Hub Island]]. It is located to the north-east of the [[Village]] center, and is reached by using a launchpad at the edge of the island.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters The Barn, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
The Barn
■ Harvest Wheat, Carrots, Potatoes, Pumpkins, and Melons.
■ Kill cows, chickens, and pigs.
■ Milk cows.
## Features
[[Carrot|Carrots]], [[Potato|Potatoes]], [[Wheat]], [[Melon|Melons]] and [[Pumpkin|Pumpkins]] grow in various places in The Barn. The Barn also summons [[Cow|Cows]], [[Pig|Pigs]] and [[Chicken|Chickens]] in the main barn building located at the center of the island and in a pen to its west.
The [[Farmhand]] is located in the barn building, an NPC that the player will need to talk to for a quest.
A launchpad to the [[Desert Settlement]] is located on the top of the terraces to the north of the barn building.
## NPCs
There are 3 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in The Barn:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|

|[[Husbandry Merchant]]|[[Husbandry Merchant]]|The Barn; 116.5, 73, -255.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells various Husbandry-related items.|✔|
|[[Windmill Operator]]|[[Windmill Operator]]|[[Windmill]]; 102.5, 82, -282.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells the same items as the [[Farm Merchant]].|✔|

# The Bastion
Aliases: Bastion
Categories: Locations
[The Bastion]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])

[Resources]

The Bastion is a location in the [[Crimson Isle]]. [[Piglin Guard|Piglin Guards]] wander around The Bastion and if they see a player, they will kick them out.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters The Bastion, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
The Bastion
■ Don't anger the guards.
## Entering
The player will have to enter The Bastion during the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] questline, to find the [[Dean]]'s stolen research papers, and again when given the Rescue Mission at the Town Board when the player is in the [[Factions|Mage Faction]]. When entering The Bastion during a quest, all abilities are disabled, [[Speed]] is capped at 100 and [[Jump Boost Potion|Jump Boost]] doesn't work.

The Bastion is a sub-location of the [[Wyld Woods]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
Located in the center of The Bastion is a wilted tree. Clicking on it grants a message, which hints at interacting with the tree with [[Muted Bark]]. Interacting with the log with purple particles emanating from it whilst the player is holding 20 [[Muted Bark]] opens up a hole in the tree, revealing an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]].
## Game Messages
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Without [[Muted Bark]]|You knock on wood... Everything stays the same!|
|With less than 20 [[Muted Bark]]|You knock on wood... Try with more bark!|
|With 20 [[Muted Bark]]|You knock on wood... Its opacity turned to 0! (It became muted...)|
## Enigma Souls
There are 3 [[Enigma Souls]] found in The Bastion:
|Name|X|Y|Z|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Tough Bark|-14.5|91|94.5|Revealed after clicking a tree with 20 [[Muted Bark]].|
|Woods Flower Pot|-5.5|60|226.5|At the end of a string of Flower Pots starting at -56.5, 67, 160.5 ([[Coordinates]]).|
|Fleespook|-26.5|89|136.5|Revealed after following the [[Fleespook]].|

# The End

[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Gregory]] [[Guber]] [[Lone Adventurer]] [[Pearl Dealer]] [[Tyzzo]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Ender Dragon]] [[End Stone Protector]] [[Enderman]] [[Endermite]] [[Nest Endermite]] [[Obsidian Defender]] [[Seer]] [[Voidling Fanatic]] [[Voidling Extremist]] [[Zealot]] [[Special Zealot]] [[Zealot Bruiser]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Aspect of the Dragons]] [[Bone Dye]] [[Combat Exp Boost]] [[Dragon Claw]] [[Dragon Essence]] [[Dragon Horn]] [[Dragon Scale]] [[Enchanted Bone]] [[Enchanted End Stone]] [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] [[Enchanted Obsidian]] [[End Stone]] [[End Stone Bow]] Ender Armor Piece ([[Ender Armor]]) [[Ender Belt]] [[Ender Cloak]] [[Ender Dragon Pet]] [[Ender Monocle]] [[Ender Necklace]] [[Ender Pearl]] [[Enderman Cortex Rewriter]] [[Enderman Pet]] [[Flint Arrow]] [[Mite Gel]] [[Obsidian]] [[Obsidian Chestplate]] Old Dragon Armor Piece ([[Old Dragon Armor]]) Old Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]) [[Pearlescent Dye]] Protector Dragon Armor Piece ([[Protector Dragon Armor]]) Protector Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]) Strong Dragon Armor Piece ([[Strong Dragon Armor]]) Strong Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]) [[Summoning Eye]] Superior Dragon Armor Piece ([[Superior Dragon Armor]]) Superior Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]) [[Travel Scroll to Dragon's Nest]] Unstable Dragon Armor Piece ([[Unstable Dragon Armor]]) Unstable Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]) Wise Dragon Armor Piece ([[Wise Dragon Armor]]) Wise Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]) Young Dragon Armor Piece ([[Young Dragon Armor]]) Young Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]])
Resources: [[End Stone]] [[Obsidian]]
The End is a location that represents the vanilla Minecraft End dimension.
Like the vanilla Minecraft End Dimension, it is home to [[Enderman|Endermen]] and the [[Ender Dragon]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters The End, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
The End
■ Fight Endermen and Endermites.
■ Mine End Stone and Obsidian.
■ Travel to the Dragon's Nest.
## Location
The End can be accessed from [[Spider's Den]] on the west side via the use of a launchpad, a player may use a portal on their island to access The End, or they may use the [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll to The End]] to access The End from anywhere. Players must have [[Combat|Combat XII (12)]] unlocked in order to access The End. The portal can be located in the [[Spider's Den]] at [[Coordinates|-382, 89, -268]].
## Features
### Mobs
The End has [[Enderman|Endermen]] all throughout it. When [[End Stone]] is mined without a [[Stonk]] or when [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] are used, there is a small chance an [[Endermite]] will spawn.
In the Dragon's Nest, [[Zealot|Zealots]], [[Obsidian Defender|Obsidian Defenders]] and [[Seer|Seers]] spawn. [[Endermite|Endermites]] also spawn naturally, as well as being spawned by [[Seer|Seers]].
In the [[Void Sepulture]], much stronger Endermen called [[Voidling Fanatic|Voidling Fanatics]] and [[Voidling Extremist|Voidling Extremists]] spawn.
### Resources
There are 3 main resources found in The End - [[Obsidian]], [[End Stone]], and [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]].
[[Obsidian]] and [[End Stone]] can be obtained from mining, and [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] will drop from slain [[Enderman|Endermen]] and [[Zealot|Zealots]].
There are three types of Zealots: normal [[Zealot|Zealots]], [[Zealot|Ender Chest Zealots]] (which drop goodies when killed), and Portal Frame or Special [[Zealot|Zealots]], which spawn rarely after killing normal or Ender Chest Zealots. They are weaker (2,000 Health) and drop [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]].
There are also [[Ender Armor]] and [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]]. Ender Armor is a rare drop from [[Enderman|Endermen]]. [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] can be obtained from special [[Zealot|Portal Frame Zealots]] that spawn rarely after killing a normal [[Zealot]] (1 in 420 chance) or after killing a [[Zealot Bruiser]] (1 in 380 chance). Zealots also drop a phoenix pet.
[[Obsidian Defender|Obsidian Defenders]] mostly drop obsidian, but they occasionally drop the [[Obsidian Chestplate]].
[[Seer|Seers]] drop arrows and rarely drop the [[End Stone Bow]].
Both of these mobs have a Pray RNGesus chance to get Rare and Epic combat EXP boosts.
There are also [[Ender Nodes]] that can be found which drop additional resources ([[Experience Bottle|Experience Bottles]], [[Grand Experience Bottle|Grand Experience Bottles]], [[End Stone]] or [[Obsidian]] (sometimes enchanted), [[Enchanted Ender Pearl|Enchanted Ender Pearls]], reforge stones, and rarely [[Shrimp the Fish]]. Watch out for Nest Endermites though, as they can easily one or two shot a newer player. They drop mite gel, which can be sold for a lot of money.
[[Pearl Dealer]] - Has a shop outside the spawn platform featuring [[Gravity]], [[End Stone]], [[Obsidian]], [[Remnant of the Eye]], [[Stonk]] and [[Silent Pearl|Silent Pearls]].
[[Guber]] - Provides a racing quest from one side of the island to another for rewards.
### Dragon's Nest
The Dragon's Nest is the boss room in The End. Its entrance is located at [[Coordinates|-600, 22, -275]]. When eight [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] are used on the altar in the center, the [[Ender Dragon]] spawns. There are seven Ender dragon types that can spawn, each one has a different amount of health, and their own [[Armor]] pieces and [[Dragon Fragment|Dragon Fragments]].
There are several sub-sections of the Dragon's nest, which contain teleport pads.
- "Dragon's View" is one, and contains a Teleport Pad to "Endertower." Dragon's View is a tall sandstone and obsidian tower. It is located at [[Coordinates|-713, 41, -317]].
### Void Sepulture
The Void Sepulture is an area in The End designed for players wanting to grind [[Enderman Slayer]] located at [[Coordinates|-576, 7, -317]]. It houses endermen with much higher health and damage, named the [[Voidling Fanatic]] and [[Voidling Extremist]]. It is recommended to only go here if you have enough gear with which you know you won't die easily because [[Remnant of the Eye|Remnants of the Eye]] do not work inside the Void Sepulture. In the Void Sepulture, Voidling Fanatics and Voidling Extremists can drop enchanted ender pearls. Voidling Extremists can also drop Enderman Cortex Rewriter.
### Zealot Bruiser Hideout
The Zealot Bruiser Hideout is an area in The End designed for stronger players to grind for [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] without cluttering the Dragon's Nest. The Zealot Bruiser Hideout is the only area in which [[Zealot Bruiser|Zealot Bruisers]] naturally spawn.

## Lore
- After the attack of the [[Jythar|Dragon Emperor]], known as [[Jythar|The Calamity]], the core of the Dragon's [[Fels|Fel Magic]] was placed on this island, which corrupted it to its today's state and created new fauna.
## Trivia
- Voidgloom Seraphim can only spawn in The End.
- The creation of the island is described in [[Journal|Dungeon Journal - Memories]].
- There is a secret room containing 2 headless armor stands, one fairy soul, and multiple chests and cobwebs. According to [[Journal|Dungeon Journal Memories]], it was [[The Wither King|Kaeman]]'s lab for studying the [[Fels|Fel Magic]] to revive his family, who have been killed by the [[Jythar|Calamity]]. He failed to revive them, however, a side effect of one of his spells merged their dead bodies with his, adding 2 heads turning him into a Wither.
  - It can be accessed by going left on the outside of the island to [[Coordinates|-660, 35, -190]] and going inside of the hole below the obsidian spike.
- The "Travel to the End" objective is only completed if the player uses the [[Launch Pad]] in the [[Spider's Den]], not if they use a portal or a warp scroll.
